## Analyzer Architecture Summary

trustyai-service-operator (also known as\: TrustyAI operator) is a Kubernetes component. managing 3 CRD(s)\: EvalHub, LMEvalJob, TrustyAIService. deploying 1 workload(s) (trustyai-service-operator-controller-manager) with 1 container(s). watching 18 resource type(s). exposing 3 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443. protected by 3 network policy/policies. runtime dependencies\: OpenTelemetry Collector. defining 25 API spec type(s) across 77 struct(s) with 330 total fields. with 564 operator config constants (31 image references, 7 env vars). following 27 reconciliation steps across 4 controller(s). reporting 15 status condition types. integrates with Istio, KServe, Kueue, management relationship via labels/annotations. deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization. kustomize overlays\: config/base, config/components/evalhub, config/components/job-mgr, config/components/lmes, config/components/nemo-guardrails, config/components/tas, config/components/webhook, config/configmaps/evalhub, config/configmaps, config/configmaps/nemo-guardrails, config/crd, config/manager, config/manifests, config/overlays/evalhub-only, config/overlays/lmes, config/overlays/mcp-guardrails, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/odh/rbac/tls, config/overlays/odh-kueue, config/overlays/rhoai, config/overlays/rhoai/rbac/tls, config/overlays/testing, config/prometheus, config/rbac-base, config/scorecard. references components\: eval-hub (import, provider), kserve (import), kube-rbac-proxy (config).
